web|url=http://www.ufc.com/event/UFC126#/fight|title=UFC® 126 Silva vs. Belfort|publisher=Ultimate Fighting Championship|date=2010-12-07|access-date=2012-09-17}}\n\n==Background==\nFormer [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Vitor Belfort]] was scheduled to fight [[Yushin Okami]] at [[UFC 122]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/20472/vitor-belfort-vs-yushin-okami-to-headlined-spike-tvs-ufc-122-event-in-germany.mma |title=Vitor Belfort vs. Yushin Okami to headline Spike TV's UFC 122 event in Germany |publisher=MMAjunkie.com |date=2010-08-29 |access-date=2012-09-17 |author=Staff |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://archive.today/20130129081614/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2010/08/vitor-belfort-vs-yushin-okami-to-headlined-spike-tvs-ufc-122-event-in-germany |archive-date=2013-01-29 }} However, UFC President [[Dana White]] said that Belfort would instead challenge [[UFC Middleweight Championship|Middleweight Champion]] [[Anderson Silva]] for the belt.{{cite web |url=http://lowkick.blitzcorner.com/UFC/Vitor-Belfort-is-officially-out-of-UFC-122-Nate-Marquardt-to-serve-as-his-replacement-10010 |title=Belfort vs Silva official; Okami to face Marquardt at UFC 122 (UPDATED) |publisher=LowKick.com |date=2010-09-22 |access-date=2012-09-17 |last=Cassidy |first=Daniel |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120203074803/http://lowkick.blitzcorner.com/UFC/Vitor-Belfort-is-officially-out-of-UFC-122-Nate-Marquardt-to-serve-as-his-replacement-10010 |archive-date=2012-02-03 }} Belfort was originally scheduled to compete against Silva on January 2, 2010, at [[UFC 108]], but due to an injury sustained by Silva, the fight had to be postponed until February 6, 2010, at [[UFC 109]] and April 10, 2010, at [[UFC 112]], respectively. However, both fights were cancelled.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/17020/anderson-silvas-recovery-slow-not-fighting-vitor-belfort-at-ufc-109.mma|title=Anderson Silva's recovery slow, not fighting Vitor Belfort at UFC 109|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2009-11-28|access-date=2012-09-17|last=Morgan|first=John|archive-date=2012-05-25|archive-url=https://archive.today/20120525144333/http://mmajunkie.com/news/17020/anderson-silvas-recovery-slow-not-fighting-vitor-belfort-at-ufc-109.mma|url-status=dead}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/17916/shoulder-injury-forces-vitor-belfort-out-of-ufc-112-title-fight-with-anderson-silva.mma|title=Shoulder injury forces Vitor Belfort out of UFC 112 title fight with Anderson Silva\n|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|last=Marrocco|first=Steven|date=2010-02-11|access-date=2012-09-17}}\n\nA bout between [[Kenny Florian]] and [[Evan Dunham]] was expected for this event. The fight was moved to [[UFC: Fight For The Troops 2]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Fight-For-Troops-January|title=Florian-Dunham Tops \"Fight for the Troops\"|publisher=Ultimate Fighting Championship|date=2010-11-11|access-date=2012-09-17}}\n\nA bout between [[Jon Fitch]] and [[Jake Ellenberger]] was expected for this event. However Fitch was pulled from the bout and instead fought former two-division [[List of UFC champions|champion]] [[B.J. Penn]] at [[UFC 127]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/21482/ufc-boss-says-fitch-vs-penn-siver-vs-sotirpoulos-booked-for-ufc-127.mma|title=UFC boss says Fitch vs. Penn, Siver vs. Sotiropoulos booked for UFC 127|date=2010-11-21|access-date=2012-09-17|last1=Morgan|last2=Stupp|first1=John|first2=Dann|author-link1=John Morgan (martial artist)|author-link2=Dann Stupp|archive-date=2012-10-23|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20121023083035/http://mmajunkie.com/news/21482/ufc-boss-says-fitch-vs-penn-siver-vs-sotirpoulos-booked-for-ufc-127.mma|url-status=dead}} Ellenberger remained on the card and instead fought [[Carlos Eduardo Rocha]].\n\n[[Sam Stout]] was originally scheduled to face [[Paul Kelly (mixed martial arts fighter)|Paul Kelly]] at this event, but dropped off of the card due to injury. [[Donald Cerrone]] stepped in to replace him, in what would become the first UFC [[Lightweight (MMA)|lightweight]] fight featuring a fighter imported from the [[World Extreme Cagefighting]] following their merger in December 2010.{{cite web|url=http://mmaweekly.com/donald-cowboy-cerrone-steps-in-to-face-paul-kelly-at-ufc-126|title=Donald \"Cowboy\" Cerrone Steps In To Face Paul Kelly At UFC Sanchez vs. Kampmann]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 127: Penn vs. Fitch''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on Sunday, February 27, 2011, at [[Acer Arena]] in Sydney, Australia.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/event/UFC127#/fight |title=UFC 127: Penn vs. Fitch|publisher=ufc.com|date=13 December 2010}} Due to the time zone difference it aired live on Saturday, February 26 in North America. This was the second UFC event held in Sydney, following the sold-out [[UFC 110]] in 2010.\n\n==Background==\nTickets for UFC Fight Club members went on sale on 14 December 2010. UFC 127 sold out moments after going on public sale on 16 December 2010, selling faster than tickets for [[UFC 110]], and making it the fastest-selling event, along with [[UFC 115]], in UFC history.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/21812/ufc-127-penn-vs-fitch-in-australia-equals-fastest-sellout-in-ufc-history.mma|title= \"UFC 127: Penn vs. Fitch\" in Australia equals fastest Live]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/22255/ufc-127s-pearson-vs-fisher-on-espn-in-u-k-no-ufc-prelims-on-spike-tv.mma|title=UFC 127's Pearson vs. Fisher on ESPN in U.K., no \"UFC Prelims\" on Spike TV|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=28 January 2012}} [[Ion Television]] aired the Pearson vs. Fisher, [[James Te Huna|Te-Huna]] vs. [[Alexander Gustafsson|Gustafsson]] and [[Nick Ring|Ring]] vs. [[Riki Fukuda|Fukuda]] preliminary bouts,{{cite Night 23''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on January 22, 2011 at [[Fort Hood]], near [[Killeen, Texas]].{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/event/fight-for-the-troops-2#/fight|title=UFC: Fight for the Troops 2|publisher=ufc.com|date=November 11, 2010}} The event was the third that the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] has hosted in cooperation with a [[United States|US]] military base, following [[UFC Fight Night 7]] and [[UFC: Fight for the Troops]].\n\n==Background==\nThe bout between [[Kenny Florian]] and [[Evan Dunham]] was expected for [[UFC 126]], but was moved to this card to serve as the main event.{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Fight-For-Troops-January|title=Florian-Dunham Headlines \"Fight for the Troops\"|publisher=ufc.com|date=November 11, 2010}} On December 6, however, Florian pulled out of the bout due to a knee injury. [[Melvin Guillard]] was pulled from a planned preliminary bout with [[Yves Edwards]] and faced Dunham in the main event.{{cite web|url=https://www.heavy.com/mma/2010/12/kenny-florian-injured-out-of-dunham-fight/|title=Kenny Florian Injured, Melvin Guillard To Face Evan Dunham|publisher=Heavy.com|date=December 6, 2010}} Edwards instead faced [[TUF 12]] alumnus [[Cody McKenzie]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/21656/replacement-cody-mckenzie-meets-yves-edwards-at-ufc-fight-night-23.mma|title=Replacement Cody McKenzie meets Yves Edwards at UFC Fight Night 23|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=December 7, 2010|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20101210114252/http://mmajunkie.com/news/21656/replacement-cody-mckenzie-meets-yves-edwards-at-ufc-fight-night-23.mma|archive-date=December 10, 2010}}\n\nOn December 30, 2010, a bout between [[Mike Swick]] and [[David Mitchell (fighter)|David Mitchell]] was called off due to Mitchell suffering an injury and Swick dealing with his Esophageal Condition.{{cite web|url=http://mmaweekly.com/david-mitchell-out-of-ufc-fight-for-the-troops-fight-with-mike-swick-due-to-injury|title=David Mitchell Out of UFC Fight For The Troops; Mike Swick Off The Card|publisher=mmaweekly.com|date=December 30, 2010}} A late replacement fight between [[Will Campuzano]] and [[Chris Cariaso]] was signed.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/21989/will-campuzano-meets-chris-cariaso-at-ufc-fight-night-23.mma|title=Will Campuzano meets Chris Cariaso at UFC Fight Night 23|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=January replaced by [[Mike Brown (fighter)|Mike Brown]].{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Brown-Replaces-Jung-Against-Yahya-at-UFC-Fight-for-the-Troops-2-29269 |title=Brown Replaces Jung Center]]\n|city=[[Louisville, Kentucky|Louisville]], [[Kentucky]]\n|attendance=8,319{{Cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/22695/ufc-on-versus-3-draws-8319-attendance-earns-471500-gate.mma|title=UFC on Versus 3 draws a reported 8,319 attendees for 471k live gate|author=John Morgan|publisher=MMA Junkie|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110307103019/http://mmajunkie.com/news/22695/ufc-on-versus-3-draws-8319-attendance-earns-471500-gate.mma|archive-date=2011-03-07}}\n|gate=$471,450\n|previousevent= [[UFC 127|UFC 127: Penn vs. Fitch]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 128|UFC 128: Shogun vs. Jones]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Live: Sanchez vs. Kampmann''''' (also known as '''''UFC on Versus 3''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on March 3, 2011, at the [[KFC Yum! Center]] in [[Louisville, Kentucky]].{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/event/UFCLive3#/fight|title=UFC on Versus: Sanchez vs. Kampmann|publisher=ufc.com|date=January 6, 2010}}{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-live-3-louisville-announcement|title=UFC Live: in Louisville March 3|publisher=ufc.com|date=January 6, 2011}} The event was broadcast on [[Versus (TV channel)|Versus]] in the U.S. and [[Rogers Sportsnet One]] in Canada. This was the first UFC event to be broadcast in 3D.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2011/02/25/ufc-on-versus-3-to-be-broadcast-in-3d|title=UFC on Versus 3 to Be Broadcast in 3D|author=Mike Chiappetta|date=25 February 2011|publisher=MMA Fighting}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event was the first UFC event hosted in [[Kentucky]].\n\n[[Matt Brown (fighter)|Matt Brown]] was expected to face Mark Scanlon at the event,{{cite web|url=https://www.heavy.com/mma/mma-news/2011/01/matt-brown-faces-mark-scanlon-at-ufc-on-versus-3|title=Matt Brown vs. Mark Scanlon At UFC on Versus 3|publisher=heavy.com|date=January 3, 2011}} but Scanlon was forced off the card{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/22096/mark-scanlon-out-at-ufc-on-versus-3-matt-brown-awaiting-new-opponent.mma|title=Mark Scanlon out at UFC on Versus 3, Matt Brown awaiting new opponent|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=January 14, 2011|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110116195757/http://mmajunkie.com/news/22096/mark-scanlon-out-at-ufc-on-versus-3-matt-brown-awaiting-new-opponent.mma|archive-date=January 16, 2011}} and was to be replaced by [[Matthew Riddle|Matt Riddle]].{{cite web|url=http://mmaweekly.com/matt-riddle-steps-in-to-face-matt-brown-at-ufc-on-versus-3-in-kentucky|title=Matt Riddle Steps in to Face Matt Brown at UFC on Versus 3 in Kentucky\n|publisher=mmaweekly.com|date=January 14, 2011}} However, the match was cancelled after Riddle injured his right hand on January 20.{{cite web|url=http://www.versus.com/blogs/mma-beat/matt-riddle-pulls-out-of-ufc-on-versus-3/|title=Matt Riddle pulls out of UFC on Versus 3|publisher=Versus.com|date=January 20, 2011}}\n\n[[Maiquel Falcao]] was due to take on [[Alessio Sakara]], but pulled out of the bout due to an injury. [[Rafael Natal]] was to step in as his replacement,{{cite web|url=http://mmaweekly.com/rafael-sapo-natal-replaces-injured-maiquel-falcao-at-ufc-on-versus-3|title=Rafael \"Sapo\" Natal Replaces Injured Maiquel Falcao at UFC on Versus 3|publisher=mmaweekly.com|date=January 14, 2011}} however on February 12, it was announced that Natal had pulled out of the bout due to a knee injury.{{cite web|url=http://www.versus.com/blogs/mma-beat/rafael-natal-withdraws-from-ufc-on-versus-3/|title=Rafael Natal withdraws from UFC on Versus 3|publisher=Versus.com|date=February 12, 2011}} Natal was replaced by [[Chris Weidman]], making his UFC debut at this event.\n{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/22466/newcomer-weidman-likely-replaces-natal-meets-sakara-at-ufc-on-versus-3.mma|title=Newcomer Weidman likely replaces Natal, meets Sakara at UFC on Versus 3|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=February 14, 2011|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://archive.today/20120713093643/http://mmajunkie.com/news/22466/newcomer-weidman-likely-replaces-natal-meets-sakara-at-ufc-on-versus-3.mma|archive-date=July 13, 2012}}\n\n[[Rousimar Palhares]] was set to face [[Alexandre Ferreira (fighter)|Alexandre Ferreira]]. However, on January 18, Ferreira was dismissed from [[Chute Boxe]], his home training camp, citing a \"lack of communication.\"{{cite web|url=http://www.fightersonlymagazine.co.uk/news/viewarticle.php?id=6008&offset=2|title=Cacareco released by Chute Boxe for \"lack of communication\"|publisher=Fighters Only|date=January 18, 2011|access-date=January 20, 2011|archive-date=January 23, 2011|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110123204418/http://www.fightersonlymagazine.co.uk/news/viewarticle.php?id=6008|url-status=dead}} Less than an hour later, it was reported that Ferreira would not be competing after losing family and friends in the [[January 2011 Rio de Janeiro floods and mudslides|Brazilian floods]], resulting in him being in \"no condition to train or fight.\"{{cite web|url=http://www.fightersonlymagazine.co.uk/news/viewarticle.php?id=6009|title=Exclusive: Palhares vs. Cacareco cancelled; Brazilian floods cited.|publisher=Fighters Only|date=January 18, 2011|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110123055509/http://www.fightersonlymagazine.co.uk/news/viewarticle.php?id=6009|archive-date=January 23, 2011}} and was replaced by [[David Branch (fighter)|David Branch]].{{cite web|url=https://www.heavy.com/mma/mma-news/2011/01/dave-branch-replaces-ferreira-faces-palhares-at-ufc-on-versus-3/|title=David Branch Replaces Ferreira, Faces Palhares At UFC on Versus 3|publisher=heavy.com|date=January 19, 2011}}\n\nA bout between [[Johny Hendricks]] and [[Paulo Thiago]] was expected for the event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/22164/johny-hendricks-vs-paulo-thiago-targeted-for-ufc-on-versus-3.mma|title=Johny Hendricks vs. Paulo Thiago targeted for UFC on Versus 3|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|access-date=January 20, 2011|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110124005901/http://mmajunkie.com/news/22164/johny-hendricks-vs-paulo-thiago-targeted-for-ufc-on-versus-3.mma|archive-date=January 24, 2011}} However, the bout was canceled after Thiago was forced out with an elbow injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/22232/paulo-thiago-scratched-from-ufc-on-versus-3-johny-hendricks-in-limbo.mma|title=Paulo Thiago scratched from UFC on Versus 3, Johny Hendricks in limbo|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|access-date=January 28, 2011|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110130222609/http://mmajunkie.com/news/22232/paulo-thiago-scratched-from-ufc-on-versus-3-johny-hendricks-in-limbo.mma|archive-date=January 30, 2011}}\n\n[[Takeya Mizugaki]] was scheduled to take on [[Francisco Rivera (fighter)|Francisco Rivera]], but on February 12, Rivera had to pull out due to an undisclosed training injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/22446/francisco-rivera-out-at-ufc-on-versus-3-takeya-mizugaki-awaits-new-opponent.mma|title=Francisco Rivera out at UFC on Versus 3, Takeya Mizugaki awaits new opponent|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=February 12, 2011|access-date=February 12, 2011|archive-date=February 15, 2011|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110215130434/http://mmajunkie.com/news/22446/francisco-rivera-out-at-ufc-on-versus-3-takeya-mizugaki-awaits-new-opponent.mma|url-status=dead}} Mizugaki instead faced UFC newcomer [[Reuben Duran]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/22469/newcomer-reuben-duran-signs-on-to-face-takeya-mizugaki-at-ufc-on-versus-3.mma|title=Newcomer Reuben Duran signs on to face Takeya Mizugaki at UFC on Versus 3|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=February 15, 2011|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120719144615/http://mmajunkie.com/news/22469/newcomer-reuben-duran-signs-on-to-face-takeya-mizugaki-at-ufc-on-versus-3.mma|archive-date=July 19, 2012}}\n\n[[Cub Swanson]] was due to take on [[Erik Koch]] at this event, but the bout was cancelled after Swanson suffered a training injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/22589/cub-swanson-out-at-ufc-on-versus-3-promotion-seeking-new-opponent-for-erik-koch.mma|title=Cub Swanson out at UFC on Versus 3, Erik Koch awaits new opponent|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=February 24, 2011|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110226163401/http://mmajunkie.com/news/22589/cub-swanson-out-at-ufc-on-versus-3-promotion-seeking-new-opponent-for-erik-koch.mma|archive-date=February 26, 2011}} Koch was instead moved to a fight with [[Raphael Assunção]] at [[UFC 128]].{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/With-Opponents-Injured-Koch-Assuncao-Tabbed-for-UFC-128-30445|title=With opponents injured, Koch/Assuncao tabbed for UFC 128|publisher=Sherdog.com|date=February 26, 2011}}\n\nThe [[Joe Stevenson|Stevenson]] vs [[Danny Castillo|Castillo]] and [[Steve Cantwell|Cantwell]] vs [[Cyrille Diabaté|Diabaté]] bouts streamed on the UFC's official [[Facebook]] page.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/22647/two-ufc-on-versus-3-prelimary-card-fights-to-stream-live-on-facebook.mma|title=Two UFC on Versus 3 preliminary-card fights to stream live on Facebook|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=March 1, 2011|access-date=March 1, 2011|archive-date=March 5, 2011|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110305022712/http://mmajunkie.com/news/22647/two-ufc-on-versus-3-prelimary-card-fights-to-stream-live-on-facebook.mma|url-status=dead}}\n\nFormer [[World Extreme Cagefighting]] play by play announcer Todd Harris called the 3D broadcast of this event, with UFC featherweight [[Kenny Florian]] on color commentary.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/news/22641/thursdays-ufc-on-versus-3-event-airs-in-3d-harris-and-florian-to-commentate.mma|title=Thursday's UFC on Versus 3 event airs in 3D, Harris and Florian to commentate|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=February 28, 2011|access-date=November 3, 2011|archive-date=April 3, 2012|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20120403084447/http://mmajunkie.com/news/22641/thursdays-ufc-on-versus-3-event-airs-in-3d-harris-and-florian-to-commentate.mma|url-status=dead}} They had previously done commentary together at [[WEC 41]] and [[WEC 49]].\n\nThe event drew an average of 681,000 viewers on [[Versus (TV channel)|Versus]].{{Cite
